# Supervisor sandbox manifest (first account)

Every file in the supervisor's scratch folder at the handover (4 Oct 2026). Our own work is committed under `files/`. Everything else is listed with the reason it stays out of this public repository. Paths are relative to the scratch folder. Third-party git clones and npm dependency folders are listed once per folder. The research notes in `docs/research/*.md` cite the sources of the excluded pages and papers; anything excluded can be fetched again from them or rebuilt with the named script.

Already moved elsewhere in the repo: the supervisor log (sanitized) is `docs/handover/supervisor/supervisor-log.md`; the queue tools and map poster are in `docs/handover/supervisor/`; the empirical audit, sample-size simulation, data measurements and transaction probe are in `research/supervisor/`.
